---
title: Calcite's source
description: The Calx modules in src/, what each matches in vanilla, and how they're tested.
---

# Calcite's source

Calcite is written in [Calx](https://calxlang.com). Module `a.b` is the file `src/a/b.calx` ([modules](https://calxlang.com/reference/modules/)). Every module that reproduces vanilla behavior is checked bit for bit by the [oracle rig](../rig/README.md).

| Module | What it is | Vanilla's |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `random.source` | `RandomSource`, the interface every random source implements; `Bits`, a source built on `next(bits)`, with the formulas built on it; the polar Gaussian; `position_seed`; `shuffle`, as `Util.shuffle` shuffles a list ([D315](../docs/decisions.md)) | `RandomSource`, `BitRandomSource`, `MarsagliaPolarGaussian`, `Mth.getSeed`, `Util.shuffle` |
| `random.legacy` | `LegacyRandom`, Java's 48-bit LCG, and `LegacyPositional` | `LegacyRandomSource`, `SingleThreadedRandomSource` and their positional factory |
| `random.xoroshiro` | `XoroshiroRandom` on xoroshiro128++, and `XoroshiroPositional`; `unmixed` and `hash_of`, a name's MD5, which a random sequence is made from ([D396](../docs/decisions.md)) | `XoroshiroRandomSource`, `Xoroshiro128PlusPlus`, `RandomSupport`'s seeding, `RandomSupport.upgradeSeedTo128bitUnmixed`, `seedFromHashOf` |
| `random.taped` | `TapedRandom`, a level's random: a `LegacyRandom` whose every value comes through its own `next`, which can write each to the RNG tape with the level and the game time ([D312](../docs/decisions.md)) | `Level.random` |
| `random.worldgen` | `WorldgenRandom<T>` over a legacy or xoroshiro source, with its chunk and feature seeding | `WorldgenRandom` |
| `random.any` | `AnyRandom` and `AnyPositional`, a legacy or a xoroshiro source or factory, whichever a world's settings pick, as worldgen holds a `RandomSource` without knowing which | `RandomSource`, `PositionalRandomFactory`, `WorldgenRandom.Algorithm.newInstance` |
| `hash.md5` | MD5, for seeding from names and for offline players' UUIDs | Guava's `Hashing.md5()` |
| `hash.crc32c` | CRC-32C, which vanilla hashes item stacks' components with ([D373](../docs/decisions.md)) | Guava's `Hashing.crc32c()` |
| `hash.ops` | `HashOps.CRC32C_INSTANCE`: each kind of value hashed with its own token before its bytes, little-endian as Guava puts them, a string by its UTF-16 units, a map's entries sorted by their hashes; and `hash_tag`, NBT hashed by its tags' own types, as `NbtOps.convertTo` gives it to `HashOps`. [`test/hash.calx`](test/hash.calx) checks it ([D373](../docs/decisions.md)) | `HashOps`, `NbtOps.convertTo` |
| `java.long_set` | fastutil's `LongOpenHashSet`: longs kept by open addressing on their mixed hash, grown as fastutil grows it, so they come out in the order vanilla's do | `it.unimi.dsi.fastutil.longs.LongOpenHashSet` |
| `java.object_set` | fastutil's `ObjectOpenHashSet` of strings: strings kept by open addressing on their mixed `hashCode`, grown as fastutil grows it, `addAll` trying for room for them all first, so they come out in the order vanilla's do, as an advancement's requirements' names do ([D534](../docs/decisions.md)). [`test/object_set.calx`](test/object_set.calx) checks eight advancements' against fastutil 8.5.18's | `it.unimi.dsi.fastutil.objects.ObjectOpenHashSet` |
| `java.text` | `string_hash`, Java's `String.hashCode` over UTF-16 code units, `compare_to`, its `compareTo`, `java_trim`, its `trim`, which trims the characters up to a space, and `split_trimmed`, Guava's `Splitter.on(c).trimResults()`, which trims Unicode's whitespace. Java's UTF-8 decoder and `BufferedReader.readLine` are Calx's `std.text.utf8.decode_lossy` and `std.io.Lines` (D156); `format_fixed`, `String.format`'s `%f`, rounded half up from `Double.toString`'s digits ([D557](../docs/decisions.md)) | `String.hashCode`, `String.compareTo`, `String.trim`, Guava's `Splitter`, `Formatter` |
| `java.date` | `to_string`, a moment as `Date.toString` writes it, and `zone_name`, the zone's name there | `java.util.Date.toString` |
| `math.mth` | `SinTable`, Minecraft's table of 65536 sines that angles turn through, and `AsinTable`, the arcsines its `atan2` starts from, each built once and passed along, as Calx keeps no global state, the sines shared by every copy ([D255](../docs/decisions.md)); `fast_inv_sqrt`, `wrap_degrees` and `floor`; `table_cos`, `Mth.cos` without the table, the entry it'd read made as the table makes it ([D356](../docs/decisions.md)), and `table_sin`, `Mth.sin` the same way ([D378](../docs/decisions.md)); `table_atan2`, `Mth.atan2` without its table at hand ([D411](../docs/decisions.md)); `RAD_TO_DEG` ([D442](../docs/decisions.md)); `wrapDegrees` in doubles, `wrap_degrees_f64` ([D497](../docs/decisions.md)); `round_float`, `Math.round` of a float ([D512](../docs/decisions.md)); `round_double`, `Math.round(double)` ([D543](../docs/decisions.md)) | `Mth.sin`, `Mth.cos`, `Mth.atan2`, `Mth.fastInvSqrt`, `Mth.wrapDegrees`, `Mth.floor` |
| `java.uuid` | `Uuid`, two `i64`s, written and read as Java writes and reads them, and as four ints as NBT keeps them; name-based (version 3) and random (version 4) UUIDs | `java.util.UUID` |
| `java.base64` | `encode`, Base64 with padding and no line breaks, and `decode`, which takes what Java's decoder takes | `java.util.Base64.getEncoder()`, `getDecoder()` |
| `java.properties` | `Properties`, a `.properties` file read with Java's rules for escapes, continued lines and comments, and written as `store` writes it | `java.util.Properties.load`, `store` |
| `java.uri` | `Uri`, a URI checked as Java's `new URI` checks it, with its messages, and `Uri.untrusted`, the links vanilla lets players open; `exception_message`, a failure's message as `getMessage` gives it, which codecs say | `java.net.URI`, `Util.parseAndValidateUntrustedUri` |
| `java.number` | `parse_int` and the rest: Java's integer parsing in radixes 2 to 36, signed and unsigned, with the messages of its `NumberFormatException`s | `Integer.parseInt`, `Long.parseLong`, `Byte`, `Short`, `parseUnsignedInt`, `parseUnsignedLong` |
| `java.character` | `is_whitespace`, the units Java counts as white space, and `code_point_of`, the code point a character's name names | `Character.isWhitespace`, `Character.codePointOf` |
| `java.names` | The names of the code points, JDK 25's, written by [`oracle/unicode/names.py`](../oracle/unicode/names.py) | `Character.getName` |
| `java.categories` | `is_non_spacing_mark` and `is_letter_or_digit`, the Unicode categories Java's `\b` asks about, JDK 25's, written by [`oracle/unicode/categories.py`](../oracle/unicode/categories.py) | `Character.getType`, `Character.isLetterOrDigit` |
| `java.regex` | `Pattern`, a Java regular expression compiled with Java's flags, failing with its messages and indexes, and matched as `Matcher.matches` matches it, backtracking in its order, or searched for as `Matcher.find` and `asPredicate` search, starting between code points where Java does; a non-spacing mark after a letter or digit is a word character for `\b`, as Java takes it. Unicode properties (`\p`, `\P`), grapheme clusters (`\X`, `\b{g}`), `CANON_EQ` and `UNICODE_CHARACTER_CLASS`'s classes fail as `unsupported` instead ([D187](../docs/decisions.md)); [`test/regex.calx`](test/regex.calx) checks cases JDK 25 answered | `java.util.regex.Pattern`, `Matcher.matches`, `Matcher.find`, `PatternSyntaxException` |
| `java.glob` | `glob_regex`, a glob turned into the regular expression Java's Unix or Windows file system turns it into, or its error; [`test/glob.calx`](test/glob.calx) checks cases JDK 25 answered | `FileSystem.getPathMatcher("glob:...")`, `Globs` |
| `java.reader` | `buffered_utf8`, a file's text as `Files.newBufferedReader(path, UTF_8)` gives it to what reads it: its UTF-16 units, and the first a read can't reach without the decoder throwing, which depends on where its 8192-char and 8192-byte pieces end | `BufferedReader`, `StreamDecoder` |
| `json.gson` | `JsonWriter`, compact JSON with Gson's escapes, and a `Json` tree as Gson's `JsonObject` writes it; `pretty`, a tree as vanilla's `new GsonBuilder().setPrettyPrinting().create()` writes the files it keeps, with HTML escapes and no `null` members; `int_value` and `long_value`, a number's `int` and `long` as `LazilyParsedNumber` reads them, and `exact_long`, its `long` as `BigDecimal.longValueExact` gives it | Gson's `JsonWriter`, with `disableHtmlEscaping` and pretty printing, `JsonElement.toString`, `LazilyParsedNumber` and `NumberLimits` |
| `json.tree` | `Json`, a JSON value, with a strict parser, a writer and lookups | |
| `json.strict` | `parse_strict`, a file's bytes read as vanilla reads the JSON it keeps, through `java.reader` with `StrictJsonParser`: what Gson's strict mode takes, and which fault it meets first, text that isn't JSON or bytes that aren't UTF-8; [`test/strict_json.calx`](test/strict_json.calx) checks it, and checked it against Gson; and `parse_lenient`, text read as `LenientJsonParser` reads it, Gson's lenient mode with its comments, single quotes, unquoted strings and empty places in arrays ([D285](../docs/decisions.md)); text that isn't JSON fails with Gson 2.14's message, what's wrong, the line, column and path, as `toString` gives it ([D369](../docs/decisions.md)) | `StrictJsonParser`, `LenientJsonParser`, Gson's `JsonReader` and `JsonParser` |
| `nbt.tag` | `Tag`, an NBT tag, read and written in network and file form with `Accounter`'s limits, and printed as SNBT, its arrays written straight into room made for them ([D259](../docs/decisions.md)), and `put_key`, `put_entry` and the rest, a compound's entries written one at a time for a writer that makes no tree ([D305](../docs/decisions.md)); its number and truth as each of vanilla's getters and codecs reads them, a list's or an array's numbers as `Codec.INT_STREAM` and `Util.fixedSize` read them, with their messages, its type's name, and its number as vanilla's log lines print it; `Tag.from_json`, JSON as `JsonOps.convertTo` makes it with `NbtOps`, each number the smallest type that holds it exactly, and `json_number_tag`, one number so; and `to_json`, a tag as JSON, for what reads JSON, such as an int provider ([D317](../docs/decisions.md)); `to_typed_json` and `from_typed_json`, a tag as JSON with each number's type kept in its text and an empty array a number of its own, which the codecs read as NBT, and `typed_number`, one number so ([D363](../docs/decisions.md)) | `Tag` and its types, `NbtIo`, `NbtAccounter`, `StringTagVisitor`, `JsonOps.convertTo` |
| `nbt.snbt` | `parse` and `parse_compound`: SNBT read into a tag, with vanilla's errors at vanilla's places, and `SyntaxError`, brigadier's message and context; `compound_at`, a compound read from the middle of a command's text, as an NBT path's patterns are ([D279](../docs/decisions.md)); `tag_at`, a tag of any type at a command's reader ([D555](../docs/decisions.md)) | `TagParser`, `SnbtGrammar`, `SnbtOperations`, the packrat parser in `util.parsing.packrat`, `CommandSyntaxException` |
| `nbt.path` | NBT paths, as commands and data packs write them, read as `NbtPathArgument` reads them ([D279](../docs/decisions.md)): `parse` and `read`, each step a `Node`, a compound's child by its name, quoted or not, matching a pattern or not, a list's element by index, every element, those matching a pattern, and the root matching one, up to a space, with brigadier's errors at vanilla's places, or what brigadier's reader throws past the end; and the path's text as `asString` gives it back; [`test/nbt_path.calx`](test/nbt_path.calx) checks each step and error, and the rig's `packs` probe paths in a pack's enchantments | `NbtPathArgument`, `NbtPathArgument.NbtPath`, brigadier's `StringReader` |
| `pack.zip` | `Zip`, a zip archive in memory: the central directory, Zip64, and entries stored or deflated, each checked against its CRC-32; `Zip.walk`, a stretch of one without its directory, file by file, with sizes from data descriptors | `java.util.zip.ZipFile`, `ZipInputStream` |
| `pack.meta` | `read_meta`, a pack's `pack.mcmeta` as vanilla reads it as it finds the pack: its description, the formats it's for checked against 26.3's, the feature flags it needs and the overlays it has for 26.3, with vanilla's warnings, each with its exception, where it can't be read; `read_filter`, its `filter` section, read again as the packs are opened, as vanilla reads it; `blocks_namespace` and `blocks_path`, what a filter hides; [`test/pack_meta.calx`](test/pack_meta.calx) checks them | `Pack.readPackMetadata`, `PackMetadataSection`, `PackFormat`, `OverlayMetadataSection`, `ResourceFilterSection`, `FeatureFlagsMetadataSection` |
| `pack.repository` | `discover`, the packs a server finds: vanilla's, its feature packs from Mojang's archive, and the world's, each folder with a `pack.mcmeta` and each `.zip` in its `datapacks` folder, made if it's missing, its links checked against `allowed_symlinks.txt`, with vanilla's lines for what isn't a pack or can't be read; `select`, those that load, picked as `configurePackRepository` picks them from `level.dat` or a new world's server.properties, with `--safeMode`, the feature flags they need, feature packs forced on and vanilla's lines; `open`, their files, with overlays and filters; `fingerprint`, what they're read from, which the boot cache is keyed by ([D190](../docs/decisions.md)); [`test/pack_repository.calx`](test/pack_repository.calx) checks them, and the rig's `packs` probe every line, pack and pick against vanilla's | `PackRepository`, `FolderRepositorySource`, `PackDetector`, `ServerPacksSource`, `BuiltInPackSource`, `MinecraftServer.configurePackRepository`, `FeatureFlags.printMissingFlags` |
| `pack.resources` | `Pack`, a pack's files under `data/`, from a zip archive, a folder of Mojang's archive or a world's folder, with its overlays stacked over it and its filter; `Resources`, the packs in order, whose `list` and `stacks` give files in vanilla's order, later packs over earlier ones, a filter hiding what's below it, and vanilla's lines for a path that can't be an identifier and a namespace that can't be one; `data`, the top pack's file of each path under some folders, as worldgen reads them ([D284](../docs/decisions.md)); and `KnownPack`; [`test/pack.calx`](test/pack.calx) checks them, and the rig's `packs` probe the files a world's packs list against vanilla's | `PackResources`, `FilePackResources`, `PathPackResources`, `OverlayedPackResources`, `MultiPackResourceManager`, `FileToIdConverter`, `KnownPack` |
| `pack.data` | `Data`, the files worldgen reads, by path: an entry of an archive or bytes from a folder, the last pack's where two have a path, sorted by path for `find`; `of_zip`, vanilla's archive alone ([D284](../docs/decisions.md)) | `MultiPackResourceManager.getResource` |
| `pack.vanilla` | `vanilla_data`: the archive of vanilla's data, with its feature packs, the stretch of Mojang's client jar that holds it, kept in `cache/`, read from an installed game or downloaded the first time, and checked against its pin; `vanilla_jar`, the same from the server jar the rig runs, and `vanilla_only`, its vanilla pack alone; `vanilla_language`, the server's language from the same data | `VanillaPackResources`, the bundler's `versions.list`, `Language.loadDefault` |
| `pack.pin` | Where the pinned server and client jars come from, their sizes and SHA-1s, and where the data is in the client jar, with its SHA-256, written by [`calx_tables.py`](../oracle/datagen/README.md#calx-modules) | |
| `registry.builtin` | The 95 built-in registries and their entries in ID order, written by [`calx_tables.py`](../oracle/datagen/README.md#calx-modules) | `BuiltInRegistries` |
| `world.modes` | `GameType` and `Difficulty`, by ID and by name, as server.properties gives them | `GameType`, `Difficulty` |
| `world.difficulty` | `LocalDifficulty`, the difficulty where a mob spawns ([D404](../docs/decisions.md)): the difficulty's ID scaled by the world's and the chunk's inhabited time and the moon's brightness, peaceful 0, with its special multiplier, `special`, and whether it's hard; `moon_brightness`, each phase's | `DifficultyInstance`, `DimensionType.MOON_BRIGHTNESS_PER_PHASE` |
| `world.dismount` | Where a mob gets up to from a bed, `findStandUpPosition`, and where it can be put down safely, `DismountHelper.findSafeDismountLocation`: the blocks around and on the bed, those that aren't dangerous first, with a floor and room for its type's box within the world border ([D497](../docs/decisions.md)) | `AbstractBedBlock.findStandUpPosition`, `DismountHelper.findSafeDismountLocation`, `EntityType.isBlockDangerous` |
| `world.pos` | `BlockPos` and `ChunkPos`, and the longs vanilla packs them into, with `BlockPos`'s `JavaHash`, `Vec3i.hashCode`, so a map of places goes over them as vanilla's does ([D348](../docs/decisions.md)) | `BlockPos`, `ChunkPos`, `Vec3i.hashCode` |
| `world.item` | `ItemStack`, an item, a count and the components it changes of its item's, its patch, each value as its codec writes it to NBT and as the network gave it; `read`, a stack read with `registry.streams` and the registries and made as vanilla's constructor makes it, and `sent`, written for a packet; and `SentStack`, a stack as a packet holds it, which a packet's `Wire`, without the registries, writes as it is ([D366](../docs/decisions.md), [CAL-77](https://acta.aaenz.no/aaen-studios/calcite#CAL-77)); `from_saved`, a stack as `ItemStack.CODEC` writes it, to send ([D372](../docs/decisions.md)); `WornSlots`, an entity's equipment as a packet sends it ([D403](../docs/decisions.md)); a merchant's offers as a packet holds them, `SentOffers` ([D490](../docs/decisions.md)) | `ItemStack`, `ItemStack.OPTIONAL_STREAM_CODEC`, `RegistryFriendlyByteBuf` |
| `world.inventory` | `Inventory`, a player's: its 36 slots and the equipment, each a stack as `ItemStack.CODEC` writes it, read from their data as `Player.readAdditionalSaveData` reads `Inventory` and `equipment`, each stack or what of it reads, a later one in a slot in place of an earlier, a slot past 35 or an equipment slot's name that isn't one left out, and a `mainhand` kept; written back as `addAdditionalSaveData` writes them, `Inventory` always and `equipment` where `PlayerEquipment.isEmpty` says there's something, which there is where the player holds something; data older than 26.3's written back as it was until a slot changes; and the inventory menu's 46 slots, crafting, armor, the 27, the hotbar and the offhand. [`test/inventory.calx`](test/inventory.calx) checks it ([D372](../docs/decisions.md)); and `EnderChest`, a player's ender chest's 27 stacks, read from `EnderItems` and written back always, as it was until it changes where it's older than 26.3 ([D383](../docs/decisions.md)); and the crafting grid's result, `result`, the inventory menu's slot 0, which isn't saved ([D386](../docs/decisions.md)) | `Inventory`, `PlayerEquipment`, `EntityEquipment`, `EquipmentSlot`, `ItemStackWithSlot`, `InventoryMenu`, `PlayerEnderChestContainer` |
| `world.menu` | The inventory menu as `InventoryMenu` and `AbstractContainerMenu` run it: `click`, `doClick`'s pickups, halves, puts, merges and swaps, shift-clicks by `quickMoveStack` onto stacks of it first and armor onto its slot, hotbar keys' swaps, a creative player's copies, double clicks gathering a stack in two passes, and drags split evenly, one each or full, each slot by its rules: nothing into the result, armor only where it's `equippable` for a player, binding keeping it on out of creative, one to an armor slot and no more than a stack stacks to; `close`, the cursor's and the crafting grid's stacks put back as `placeItemBackInInventory` puts them; `Effect`s for what else happens, the crafting grid telling the menu, equip sounds and stacks put back; and what the server knows of the client's slots, `RemoteSlot.Synchronized`, a stack it sent or a `HashedStack` the client gave, so `changes` sends only what the client hasn't got. Throwing out waits for item entities, and bundles' own clicks for bundles. [`test/menu.calx`](test/menu.calx) checks it ([D374](../docs/decisions.md)); `add_item`, `Player.addItem`, and `contains_like`, `Inventory.contains`, which a block's use gives through ([D381](../docs/decisions.md)); and containers' menus over `Slots`, the container's stacks then the player's 36, `ChestMenu`'s, `ShulkerBoxMenu`'s, `DispenserMenu`'s and `HopperMenu`'s: their shift-clicks into the container from its first slot and out of it into the player's from the hotbar's last, a shulker box's slots refusing shulker boxes, `Effect.Changed` for a container's slot that changed, which the server saves, and `transfer_from`, `transferState`, what the client was sent of the player's slots kept by the inventory menu as a container's closes ([D383](../docs/decisions.md)); and the inventory menu's crafting grid: its result made at each change, `Effect.Grid`, which the server sends with the next state, taken whole, with `ResultSlot.onTake`'s using up and leftovers, shift-clicked for as long as the grid makes the same, what doesn't fit dropped, swapped out with a hotbar key, and never onto a cursor holding something else ([D386](../docs/decisions.md)); and a crafting table's menu, `CRAFTING_MENU`, over its result and 3 by 3 grid, which make their result as the inventory's grid does, with `CraftingMenu.quickMoveStack`'s shift-clicks, into the grid from the player's slots among them, and its grid put back as it closes ([D387](../docs/decisions.md)); `same_components`, public for merging items ([D389](../docs/decisions.md)); `Effect.Thrown`, a stack thrown, its thrower kept; a click outside the menu throwing what the cursor carries, all or one, and the throw key, one of a slot's stack or all, as `doClick` has them; `pick_up`, `Inventory.add` for an item picked up ([D390](../docs/decisions.md)); a merchant's menu, `MERCHANT_MENU`: its two payments, what its trade gives, which takes nothing placed, its shift-clicks, nothing taken by a double-click, and its payments put back as it's closed ([D490](../docs/decisions.md)); trades in a merchant's menu: what its payments buy, `update_sell`, as each is set and changed, by the picked offer or the first they buy, either way round, priced by demand and special price; what the trade gives taken only where they buy it, which trades, the payments paid and the offer used, `merchant_take`, its shift-click trading till they run out; a trade picked, `select_trade`, the payments moved back and filled from the inventory; and the merchant told, `TradeUpdated` and `Traded` ([D491](../docs/decisions.md)); a beacon's menu, `BEACON_MENU`: its payment slot, which takes one of `#beacon_payment_items`, its shift-click and its payment dropped as it closes; `holds_own`, the menus that hold their own slots; and a drop the server's alone, `Effect.Dropped`'s flag ([D521](../docs/decisions.md)); `broadcastChanges` slot by slot, as vanilla's goes: each slot's listeners told where it's changed since they last were, `triggerSlotListeners` with `lastSlots`, `listen`, then the client sent it where it differs from what it has, `synchronizeSlotToRemote`, `sync_slot`, then the carried stack, `sync_carried`; `new_listener`, a new menu's listener, told of each slot; `of_inventory`, whether a menu's slot is the player's inventory's ([D531](../docs/decisions.md)); the recipe that makes a crafting grid's result kept as the one used, `setRecipeUsed`, which the menu keeps between clicks, `ResultContainer.recipeUsed`; and the result's `checkTakeAchievements` as it's taken, and as each of a shift-click's crafts moves out, `onQuickCraft`: the recipe used with the grid as it is, `Effect.Crafted`, then let go where it isn't special ([CAL-164](https://acta.aaenz.no/aaen-studios/calcite#CAL-164)). [`test/menu.calx`](test/menu.calx) takes and shift-clicks results ([D535](../docs/decisions.md)); `Effect.Made`, the item a result slot gives and how many it counts, `removeCount`, as vanilla counts them ([D545](../docs/decisions.md)); `add_item` lets infinite materials take what's left where its last pass puts none in, as vanilla's `Inventory.add` does ([D549](../docs/decisions.md)); `with_count` public, `copyWithCount` ([D559](../docs/decisions.md)) | `InventoryMenu`, `AbstractContainerMenu`, `Slot`, `ArmorSlot`, `RemoteSlot`, `HashedStack`, `HashedPatchMap`, `Inventory.add`, `Inventory.placeItemBackInInventory`, `ChestMenu`, `ShulkerBoxMenu`, `ShulkerBoxSlot`, `DispenserMenu`, `HopperMenu`, `AbstractContainerMenu.transferState`, `ResultSlot`, `ResultContainer`, `TransientCraftingContainer`, `AbstractCraftingMenu.slotChangedCraftingGrid`, `CraftingMenu`, `MerchantMenu`, `MerchantContainer.updateSellItem`, `MerchantOffers.getRecipeFor`, `MerchantOffer.satisfiedBy`, `MerchantOffer.take`, `MerchantResultSlot`, `MerchantMenu.tryMoveItems`, `BeaconMenu`, `ContainerListener`, `triggerSlotListeners`, `synchronizeSlotToRemote`, `ResultSlot.checkTakeAchievements`, `RecipeCraftingHolder.awardUsedRecipes`, `Slot.onQuickCraft`, `Effect.Made` |
| `world.attributes` | A player's attributes as `AttributeMap` and `AttributeInstance` keep them: each the player has, `DefaultAttributes`', made as it's first asked for, which marks it changed, with its base and its modifiers in the order they came; its value, each `ADD_VALUE` added, then each `ADD_MULTIPLIED_BASE` of that, then each `ADD_MULTIPLIED_TOTAL` multiplied in, each operation's in the order fastutil's open hash map of them keeps, then kept in its range; those changed since they were sent, which are sent to clients; `pack` and `apply`, as `Packed.LIST_CODEC` writes and reads them; a stack's modifiers in a slot, its `attribute_modifiers`' for groups the slot's in and its enchantments' `attributes` effects, `LevelBasedValue`s in floats; `equip`, as `collectEquipmentChanges` and `handleHandSwap` take an old stack's off and put a new one's on; `update_player`'s creative ranges, and the waypoint range a crouching player loses ([D380](../docs/decisions.md)); and `sprint`. | `world.entity_data` | An entity's synced data as `SynchedEntityData` keeps it ([D380](../docs/decisions.md)): each value by its ID, with its serializer's ID, set only where it changes, a float by its bits, and whether it's changed since it was sent; `set_flag`, a shared flag's bit; `pack_dirty`, the changed ones as `ClientboundSetEntityDataPacket` writes them, in ID order, ending in 255; and `player_data`, a player's 21 values with `Entity`'s, `LivingEntity`'s, `Avatar`'s and `Player`'s defaults, with the shared flags', poses' and serializers' IDs. a mob's flags, an ageable mob's baby and age lock and a mooshroom's type, `mooshroom_data` ([D400](../docs/decisions.md)); the farm animals' variant serializers, `cow_data`, `pig_data`, `chicken_data` and `sheep_data` ([D401](../docs/decisions.md)); a horse's and a donkey's, `horse_data` and `donkey_data` ([D402](../docs/decisions.md)); the creeper's, zombie's, skeleton's, spider's and slime's values ([D404](../docs/decisions.md)); a villager's data, `Value.Villager`, and `redefine`, a value defined again as a type draws it ([D405](../docs/decisions.md)); a zombie horse's values ([D406](../docs/decisions.md)); `Value.Name`, a text component in an optional, the custom name ([D441](../docs/decisions.md)); an arrow's values, `arrow_data`: its flags, crit and no physics, its pierce level, whether it's in the ground and its color ([D458](../docs/decisions.md)); a villager's, `villager_data` ([D483](../docs/decisions.md)); a block's place in an optional, `Value.Pos` ([D497](../docs/decisions.md)); `Value.Particles`, a living entity's effect particles, a coloured one never equal to another, as `ColorParticleOption` keeps `Object.equals` ([D509](../docs/decisions.md)); | `world.block_use` | What a use on a block comes to before what's held is used, as vanilla's blocks have it ([D378](../docs/decisions.md)): `Used`, whether it passed, falls to the empty hand's use or took the click; `User`, what of the player and their stack the blocks read; `use_item_on`, `useItemOn` of the classes that have one, redstone ore lit, cauldrons, a beehive, cake, a campfire's food, candles, a chiseled bookshelf, a composter, a copper golem statue, a decorated pot, a flower pot, a jukebox, a lectern, a note block, a pumpkin, a respawn anchor, a shelf, signs, a sweet berry bush, TNT and a vault; and `use_without_item`, `useWithoutItem`, the blocks with a menu taking the click, a moving piston with nothing to finish it taken away, beds, bells hit right, buttons, cake, cave vines, game master blocks, comparators, repeaters and daylight detectors, composters, doors and trapdoors but iron ones, a dragon egg, fence gates, flower pots, jukeboxes, lecterns, levers, light, note blocks, redstone wire on its own, respawn anchors, signs and sweet berries. Whether the click's taken is vanilla's, so a block placed against another goes where vanilla's would. A use by hand does what vanilla's does ([D379](../docs/decisions.md)): a door, trapdoor or fence gate swung, the gate opened away from the player, with their sounds for all but them; a lever pulled and a button pressed, with the blocks around told and the button's tick to let go; a repeater's delay, a comparator's mode and a daylight detector turned; redstone dust turned between a dot and a cross; a note block tuned and played; a bell hit right rung; and a light block's level turned by a game master. Items are used on blocks as vanilla's are ([D381](../docs/decisions.md)): cauldrons filled and emptied by buckets and bottles, washing a stack's dye, a banner's last pattern or a shulker box's color off; composters filled by `compostable`'s layers and emptied; flower pots planted and emptied; candles put out; candles put on cakes, and cakes eaten; books put on lecterns; books put in and taken from a chiseled bookshelf's slots by where it's hit; and stacks put in decorated pots, which wobble. What each does to the player, a stack spent, filled, changed or given or food eaten, is a `Handed` for `server.network` to do. Jukeboxes take records and give them back, and shelves swap stacks by the slot hit or, powered, a chain's with the hotbar, `Handed.Swapped` and `Handed.Slot`, as `User.hotbar` has it; a decorated pot puffs its dust, and a block entity's `setChanged` tells the comparators by it; a use vanilla answers with `SUCCESS_SERVER` is a `Handed.Swing`; and a candle cake eaten draws where its candle would pop ([D382](../docs/decisions.md)). A container's use opens its menu, `Handed.Open`, a chest's, a barrel's, an ender chest's, a shulker box's, a dispenser's, a dropper's or a hopper's, as `world.containers` finds it ([D383](../docs/decisions.md)). What other uses do, as another menu opened, is told of as missing, but redstone ore's light and a moving piston's going. [`test/block_use.calx`](test/block_use.calx) checks the uses by hand, and [`test/item_use.calx`](test/item_use.calx) the items'; and a crafting table's menu opened ([D387](../docs/decisions.md)); redstone ore lit through `interact_ore` ([D431](../docs/decisions.md)); `proper_bell_hit`, whether a bell's met on a side that rings it, by a player's use or an arrow ([D478](../docs/decisions.md)); a full composter's bone meal dropped where the level's random puts it, `extractProduce`, and its layers' draws public, `compost_layers` ([D496](../docs/decisions.md)); a beacon opening its menu ([D521](../docs/decisions.md)); `Handed.Stat`, a statistic a use counts: containers opened, bells rung, cakes eaten, note blocks tuned, plants potted, records played, cauldrons filled, used and washed, and the `ITEM_USED` of what's put in or on a block ([D547](../docs/decisions.md)) | `BlockBehaviour.useItemOn`, `BlockBehaviour.useWithoutItem` and their overrides, `InteractionResult`, `DoorBlock`, `TrapDoorBlock.toggle`, `FenceGateBlock`, `LeverBlock.pull`, `ButtonBlock.press`, `RepeaterBlock`, `ComparatorBlock`, `DaylightDetectorBlock`, `RedStoneWireBlock.updatesOnShapeChange`, `NoteBlock`, `BellBlock.onHit`, `LightBlock`, `CauldronInteractions`, `LayeredCauldronBlock`, `ComposterBlock`, `FlowerPotBlock`, `CandleBlock`, `CandleCakeBlock`, `CakeBlock`, `LecternBlock`, `ChiseledBookShelfBlock`, `SelectableSlotContainer`, `DecoratedPotBlock`, `ItemUtils.createFilledResult`, `JukeboxBlock`, `JukeboxPlayable.tryInsertIntoJukebox`, `JukeboxBlockEntity`, `ShelfBlock`, `ShelfBlockEntity`, `ChestBlock.useWithoutItem`, `BarrelBlock.useWithoutItem`, `EnderChestBlock.useWithoutItem`, `ShulkerBoxBlock.useWithoutItem`, `DispenserBlock.useWithoutItem`, `HopperBlock.useWithoutItem`, `Handed.Stat` |
| `world.containers` | Containers' menus as their blocks open them ([D383](../docs/decisions.md)): `opening`, the menu a use on a chest, a trapped or copper chest, a barrel, an ender chest, a shulker box, a dispenser, a dropper or a hopper opens, its menu type, its title, a custom name's or its block's, and its block entities' slots, `Part`s; none where a chest's lid is under a redstone conductor or an ender chest's is, a shulker box's lid has no room to open, `canOpen`, or a container's loot is to come or it's locked, which are told of as missing; `chest_halves`, a chest and the half it's joined to, the right half's slots first, as `ChestBlock.combine` and `CompoundContainer` have them, and titled `container.chestDouble` unless a half's named; `items_of` and `put_items`, the stacks read from the block entities' `Items` and written back; `start_open` and `stop_open`, `ContainerOpenersCounter`'s `incrementOpeners` and `decrementOpeners`: the first opener's sound, a chest's from between its halves and its left half's none, the lid's block event, a barrel's `open` set, a trapped chest's neighbours and those under it told, a shulker box's own count with its block event every time, and the recheck scheduled 5 ticks on; `recheck`, `recheckOpeners`, the players within range who have it open counted again, every 5 ticks while there's one; and a shulker box's lid, `shulker_event` and `shulker_tick`, opening and closing a tenth each tick, its neighbours told as it starts and ends. [`test/mob.calx`](test/mob.calx) checks it; each biome's villager type, `villager_types` and `villager_type_of` ([D405](../docs/decisions.md)); each kind's hurt and death sounds ([D416](../docs/decisions.md)) | `VariantUtils.selectVariantToSpawn`, `PriorityProvider`, `SpawnContext`, `VillagerType.byBiome` |
| `world.entities` | A level's item entities: `Entities`, in the order they came, which is the tick list's, and by section in the order they came into each; `find`, those whose boxes meet a box in `getEntities`' order; `tick`, each that ticks, merging the smaller of two stacks that fit into the larger; `remove` and `take_gone`, the items gone for their trackers; `EntityTicking`, which chunks tick entities, and `StackSizes`, a stack's most ([D389](../docs/decisions.md)); `begin_tick`, `tick_next` and `end_tick`, the tick list run a player at a time, so the items that came in before a player tick before it, and `gone_after`, the sound each going came after; `lend` and `give_back`, an item taken out for its tracker and put back; `place` ([D390](../docs/decisions.md)); `chunks` and `in_chunk`, the items by chunk, in the order `storeChunkSections` saves them ([D391](../docs/decisions.md)); experience orbs among the items, `Entity`, in the one tick list, with `add_orb`, `orb`, `lend_orb` and `give_back_orb`, `find_orbs` and `find_any`, `scan_merges`, an orb taking in those of its value 40 IDs on, and `merge_award`, `tryMergeToExisting` ([D397](../docs/decisions.md)); maps whose keys show in their messages as enum names or description IDs, and `promotePartial`; and the registries vanilla loads with a server's resources, such as predicates and context providers, which can't be named while the others load, only given in full ([D193](../docs/decisions.md)); and an exception a codec throws, which ends the read there ([D279](../docs/decisions.md)); a record keeps a partial value when an optional field doesn't read ([D281](../docs/decisions.md)); an `Either` of records read into the one that holds it, and a dispatched map whose values' codec depends on the key ([D360](../docs/decisions.md)); and `frozen`, for registries that are complete, where an entry or tag that isn't there fails as it's read ([D362](../docs/decisions.md)); and `nbt`, which reads a tag's typed JSON as `NbtOps` reads NBT, its numbers, truth and arrays, with its messages, through `int_of`, `not_map`, `shown` and the rest, which the custom readers use too ([D363](../docs/decisions.md)); and `writing`, for values off the network, which vanilla holds as they came, checked as a codec checks one it writes ([D365](../docs/decisions.md)); and `defaults_bound`, items' default components read as a stack asks for them ([D367](../docs/decisions.md)); `Matcher`, `createLegacyComponentMatcher`, a record of the kind a field names or else the first that reads in full, and `Late`, a codec found by its name as it reads ([D368](../docs/decisions.md)); and `hash`, a value as its codec writes it to NBT, hashed as the codec writes it to `HashOps`, by the codec's own types, so a `Codec.BOOL`'s byte is a boolean and a list's ints a list, an alternative picked by reading the value back, and an item stack's or a patch's written out by hand by its record ([D373](../docs/decisions.md));
